Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文预测LiV₂F₆同时具有交变磁性和电荷序诱导的铁电性，可实现超快电控磁性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索同时具有交变磁性和铁电性的材料，以实现超快电控磁性，为未来电子器件设计提供新平台

Method: 结合对称性分析和第一性原理电子结构计算，预测LiV₂F₆的材料特性；通过时间依赖密度泛函理论计算极化反转动力学

Result: LiV₂F₆同时具有交变磁性和电荷序诱导的铁电性，表现出强磁电耦合；极化反转可在15飞秒内完成，实现超快电控磁性

Conclusion: LiV₂F₆为实现超快电控磁性提供了有前景的材料平台，对设计未来电子器件具有重要意义

Abstract: The altermagnetism with antiparallel spin alignment exhibits anisotropic spin splitting and may possess an insulating state with a high Neel temperature, while the charge-order-induced ferroelectricity has ultrafast electric polarization switching. Considering that altermagnetism requires breaking space inversion,, the physical foundation for exploring ultrafast electrically controlled magnetism in altermagnetic ferroelectric materials is thus established. In this Letter, based on symmetry analysis and first-principles electronic structure calculations, we predict that LiV$_2$F$_6$ is a material that simultaneously hosts altermagnetism and charge-order-induced ferroelectricity. Since both the altermagnetism and ferroelectricity originate from charge order, LiV$_2$F$_6$ should exhibit strong magnetoelectric coupling. Our calculations indeed demonstrate that electric polarization reversal can induce band spin-polarization switching in LiV$_2$F$_6$. Moreover, time-dependent density functional theory calculations show that the electric polarization reversal in LiV$_2$F$_6$ occurs in 15 femtoseconds. Consequently, ultrafast electrically controlled magnetism can be realized in LiV$_2$F$_6$. Given that LiV$_2$F$_6$ has already been experimentally synthesized, our work provides a promising material platform for achieving ultrafast electrically controlled magnetism, which might have significant implications for the design of future electronic devices.

</details>


### [95] [Formation of Ag and Au Plasmonic Nanoparticles by Ion Implantation in Ga$_2$O$_3$ thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23110)
*Inês Freitas,Ana Sofia Sousa,Duarte Magalhães Esteves,Mamour Sall,Ângelo Rafael Granadeiro da Costa,Joana Madureira,Sandra Cabo Verde,Katharina Lorenz,Marco Peres*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次通过离子注入在Ga₂O₃薄膜中形成等离子体银和金纳米颗粒，研究了退火温度对局域表面等离子体共振的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ga₂O₃作为宽带隙半导体在光电子和传感应用中具有优异性能，但需要开发将等离子体纳米结构集成到Ga₂O₃中的可行方法

Method: 采用射频溅射在蓝宝石衬底上沉积Ga₂O₃薄膜，通过150 keV能量、5×10¹⁶ ions/cm²注量的Ag或Au离子注入，随后在200-700°C温度范围内进行热退火处理

Result: RBS测量显示注入过程中的饱和效应和退火后的外扩散；TEM证实了金属纳米颗粒的形成；Ag注入薄膜在注入态即显示明显的LSPR带，随退火温度升高红移；Au注入薄膜仅在≥500°C退火后才出现明显的LSPR峰

Conclusion: 离子注入是集成等离子体纳米结构到Ga₂O₃中的可行方法，观察到的LSPR位移主要归因于Ga₂O₃基体的变化而非纳米颗粒尺寸变化

Abstract: Gallium oxide (Ga$_2$O$_3$) is a wide-bandgap semiconductor with exceptional electrical and optical properties, making it a promising material for optoelectronic and sensing applications. In this work, we demonstrate for the first time the formation of plasmonic silver (Ag) and gold (Au) nanoparticles embedded in Ga$_2$O$_3$ thin films via ion implantation. Ga$_2$O$_3$ films deposited by RF sputtering on sapphire substrates were implanted with Ag or Au ions at 150 keV and a nominal fluence of 5 $\times$ 10$^{16}$ ions/cm$^2$, followed by thermal annealing between 200 and 700 °C. Rutherford backscattering spectrometry (RBS) measurements revealed saturation effects during implantation, resulting in lower incorporated fluences, as well as out-diffusion with post-implantation annealing. Transmission electron microscopy confirmed the formation of metallic nanoparticles with a distribution consistent with the metal profiles measured by RBS. Optical absorption measurements showed a pronounced localized surface plasmon resonance (LSPR) band in the Ag-implanted films, visible even in the as-implanted state and red-shifting with increasing annealing temperature, while Au-implanted films exhibited a distinct LSPR peak only after annealing at $\geq$500 °C. The observed LSPR shifts with annealing are attributed primarily to changes in the Ga$_2$O$_3$ matrix rather than a change in nanoparticle size. These results establish ion implantation as a viable approach for integrating plasmonic nanostructures into Ga$_2$O$_3$.

</details>


### [96] [Fine-tuning of universal machine-learning interatomic potentials for 2D high-entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23029)
*Chun Zhou,Hannu-Pekka Komsa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）模型在二维过渡金属硫化物高熵合金（2D-HEAs）中的适用性，并开发了有效的微调策略，使模型在预测混合能方面达到接近DFT的精度，同时支持大规模蒙特卡洛模拟和随机结构采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金（HEAs）及其二维对应物（2D-HEAs）因其可调性质和催化潜力而受到关注，但其化学复杂性使得直接密度泛函理论（DFT）计算计算成本过高。这种复杂性也使机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）的训练具有挑战性，但可以通过使用通用MLIPs作为起点来克服。

Method: 系统生成和选择训练结构，研究通用MLIP模型在二维过渡金属硫化物HEAs中的适用性，并开发有效的微调策略。将通用模型和微调模型的性能与DFT进行基准测试，应用于实验合成的(Mo,Ta,Nb,W,V)S2体系。

Result: 研究中使用的所有通用MLIPs在没有微调的情况下都无法得到令人满意的混合能预测结果。基于枚举结构微调的模型在预测混合能方面可以达到接近DFT的精度，同时能够进行DFT无法实现的大规模蒙特卡洛模拟和随机结构采样。

Conclusion: 通用MLIPs需要针对特定体系进行微调才能获得准确结果。通过适当的微调策略，MLIPs可以成为研究复杂2D-HEAs体系的有力工具，克服DFT计算限制，实现大规模模拟和采样。

Abstract: High-entropy alloys (HEAs) and their two-dimensional counterparts (2D-HEAs) have recently attracted attention due to their tunable properties and catalytic potential, yet their chemical complexity makes direct density functional theory (DFT) calculations computationally prohibitive. The complexity also makes training of machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) challenging, but this could possibly be overcome by employing universal MLIPs as starting point. In this work, we investigate the applicability of universal MLIP models for 2D transition metal sulfide HEAs and develop effective fine-tuning strategies. Training structures are systematically generated and selected, and the performance of universal and fine-tuned models are benchmarked against DFT. We find that all universal MLIPs employed in this work yield unsatisfactory mixing energies without fine-tuning. Applied to the experimentally synthesized (Mo,Ta,Nb,W,V)S$_2$ system, fine-tuned models based on enumerated structures can achieve near-DFT accuracy in predicting mixing energies while enabling Monte-Carlo simulations and random structure sampling at scales inaccessible to DFT.

</details>


### [97] [Open Quantum System Theory of Muon Spin Relaxation in Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23137)
*Elvis F. Arguelles,Osamu Sugino*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种非马尔可夫μ子自旋弛豫理论，将植入μ子视为与时间相关的局域磁场环境耦合的开放量子自旋系统，使用Schwinger-Keldysh影响泛函方法推导出包含有色涨落和延迟记忆扭矩的自旋随机运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统μSR分析通常基于马尔可夫近似或强碰撞近似，无法充分处理非马尔可夫效应和延迟记忆效应。需要发展超越这些近似的理论框架，以更精确地分析零场和弱纵向场μSR谱，特别是对于具有复杂磁环境的材料系统。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh影响泛函表述，将植入μ子建模为开放量子自旋系统，推导出自旋随机运动方程。该方程同时包含有色涨落和延迟记忆扭矩项，在适当极限下可简化为标准Kubo-Toyabe描述。理论能够进行超越强碰撞近似的全局分析。

Result: 应用于Li₀.₇₃CoO₂系统时，成功分离了静态和锂驱动分量，发现了中间温度窗口内的热激活涨落速率，并在ZF/弱LF线形中识别出明显的非马尔可夫特征，这些特征由延迟反馈/记忆核捕获。

Conclusion: 该非马尔可夫理论为μSR分析提供了更精确的框架，能够处理复杂磁环境中的时间相关性效应，成功应用于LiCoO₂系统并揭示了传统方法无法捕捉的非马尔可夫特征，为材料磁性质研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We present a non-Markovian theory of muon spin relaxation that treats the implanted muon as an open quantum spin coupled to a temporally correlated local magnetic environment. Using a Schwinger-Keldysh influence-functional formulation, we derive a spin stochastic equation of motion in which colored fluctuations and retarded memory torque appear on equal footing. In the appropriate limits, the theory reduces to standard Kubo-Toyabe descriptions. This enables quantitative, global analysis of zero-field (ZF) and weak longitudinal-field (LF) $μ$SR spectra beyond the strong-collision approximation. Applied to $\mathrm{Li}_{0.73}\mathrm{CoO}_2$ when the muon is frozen at the stopping site, the approach separates static and Li-driven components, finds a thermally activated fluctuation rate over the intermediate-temperature window, and identifies a clear non-Markovian signature in the ZF/weak-LF line shapes captured by a retarded backaction/memory kernel.

</details>


### [98] [Field-induced spin-flip and spin-flop transitions in NdFeO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23165)
*Mariana M. Gomes,Rui Vilarinho,E. Miranda,Ana S. Silva,Christelle Kadlec,Filip Kadlec,Miroslav Lebeda,Petr Proschek,Matus Mihalik,Marian Mihalik,Diparkan Jana,Fadi Choueikani,Clement Faugeras,José Antonio Paixão,Ester de Prado,Stanislav Kamba,Joaquim Agostinho Moreira*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究展示了在NdFeO3中，外加磁场方向驱动不同磁相变序列，揭示了4f-3d各向异性耦合如何实现磁场对耦合自旋激发的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土正铁氧体中的稀土4f和过渡金属3d磁矩之间的交换耦合产生竞争性磁相互作用和多个亚稳态，为开发下一代自旋技术提供了有趣平台。研究旨在探索磁场方向如何驱动不同磁相变序列。

Method: 使用拉曼光谱和偏振太赫兹光谱技术，辅以磁化强度和比热测量，追踪不同磁相的温度和磁场依赖性演化，以及由4f-3d磁各向异性相互作用驱动的连续自旋重排。

Result: 当磁场沿晶体学c轴施加时，自旋重取向转变后跟随自旋翻转和自旋翻转过程，在低温下产生异常复杂的磁相序列。低于8K时，与Nd亚晶格有序相关的预效应显著改变了转变路径。

Conclusion: 各向异性4f-3d耦合使磁场能够控制耦合自旋激发，为在稀土正铁氧体中访问新颖自旋构型提供了途径。

Abstract: Magnetic control of correlated spin systems is central to the development of next-generation spin-based technologies. Rare-earth orthoferrites provide an interesting platform in which exchange coupling between rare-earth 4f and transition-metal 3d moments generates competing magnetic interactions and multiple metastable states. Here, we show that the orientation of the applied magnetic field drives different magnetic phase transition sequences in NdFeO3 across a broad temperature range. Using Raman and polarized terahertz spectroscopies, supported by magnetization and specific-heat measurements, we track the temperature- and field-dependent evolution of the different magnetic phases and the successive spin rearrangements, driven by 4f - 3d magnetic anisotropic interactions. For fields applied along the crystallographic c-axis, a spin-reorientation transition is followed by spin-flop and spin-flip processes, producing an unexpectedly complex magnetic phase sequence at low temperatures. Below 8 K, precursor effects associated with ordering of the Nd-sublattice strongly modify the transition pathway. Our results demonstrate how anisotropic 4f-3d coupling enables magnetic-field control of coupled spin excitations and provide a route to accessing novel spin configurations in rare-earth orthoferrites.

</details>


### [99] [Virtual materials testing of ASSB cathodes combining AI-based stochastic 3D modeling and numerical simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23248)
*Anina Dufter,Sabrina Weber,Orkun Furat,Johannes Schubert,René Rekers,Maximilian Luczak,Erik Glatt,Andreas Wiegmann,Anja Bielefeld,Volker Schmidt*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种虚拟材料测试框架，通过结合图像分析、随机微观结构建模和数值模拟来优化全固态电池阴极的微观结构，建立几何描述符与宏观性能之间的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全固态电池阴极的性能强烈依赖于其微观结构，优化阴极形态可以增强离子和电子电导率等宏观性能。传统物理实验成本高且耗时，需要开发虚拟测试方法来减少实验需求并深入理解结构-性能关系。

Method: 整合图像分析和随机微观结构建模生成大量真实的3D微观结构，通过数值模拟评估其宏观性能。建立参数回归模型，纳入体积分数、平均测地线曲折度、比表面积和收缩度等几何描述符。系统变化模型参数生成广泛的微观结构，同时保持接近原始阴极形态。

Result: 构建了包含多种微观结构的数据集，能够校准回归模型。通过比较预测和模拟的有效性能（如离子和电子电导率），评估了回归模型的预测性能，量化了几何描述符组合解释和预测宏观性能变化的准确性。

Conclusion: 虚拟材料测试框架为全固态电池阴极提供了定量洞察微观结构如何影响材料性能的方法。通过几何描述符与宏观性能的关联，该方法能够指导阴极微观结构的优化设计，减少对物理实验的依赖。

Abstract: The performance of all-solid-state battery (ASSB) cathodes strongly depends on their microstructure. Optimizing the cathode morphology can therefore enhance effective macroscopic properties such as ionic and electronic conductivity. The search for optimized microstructures can be facilitated by virtual materials testing: By integrating image analysis and stochastic microstructure modeling to generate a wide range of realistic 3D microstructures and evaluate their effective macroscopic properties by means of numerical simulations, thereby reducing the need for extensive physical experiments. This approach allows for the investigation of structure-property relationships through parametric regression models that incorporate relevant geometrical descriptors of microstructures such as volume fractions, mean geodesic tortuosities, specific surface areas, and constrictivities. By linking these geometrical descriptors to macroscopic properties, virtual materials testing provides quantitative insight into how microstructure influences material performance. In the present paper, this framework is applied for ASSB cathodes. In addition, by systematically varying model parameters, a broad range of 3D microstructures can be generated, which remain close to the original cathode morphology while inducing targeted changes in selected geometrical descriptors. The resulting database enables the calibration of regression models whose predictive performance is assessed by comparing predicted and simulated effective properties such as the ionic and electronic conductivity, thereby quantifying how accurately combinations of geometrical descriptors can explain and predict variations in effective macroscopic properties.

</details>


### [100] [Experimental Insights into the Limiting Mechanism of Vacancy Transport in Sodium Metal Anodes for Solid State Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23340)
*Ansgar Lowack,Rafael Anton,Bingchen Xue,Kristian Nikolowski,Cornelius Dirksen,Mareike Partsch,Alexander Michaelis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究钠金属陶瓷固态电池中放电时Na/陶瓷界面空位积累导致电极脱层的机制，发现临界电流密度受界面热力学而非体扩散限制，提出亲钠导电中间层和低张力界面是稳定高倍率电池的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钠金属陶瓷固态电池相比传统二次电池具有更高安全性和能量密度，但放电时Na/陶瓷界面空位积累导致的电极脱层问题尚未被理解和解决，这限制了其实际应用。

Method: 采用线性电流斜坡法，在两个钠金属电极间施加电流以诱发空位积累；研究三种不同陶瓷固态电解质（Na₁.₉Al₁₀.₆₇Li₀.₃₃O₁₇、Na₃.₄Zr₂Si₂.₄P₀.₆O₁₂、Na₅SmSi₄O₁₂）的临界电流密度温度依赖性；通过Arrhenius分析获得活化能；引入薄锡-钠合金中间层研究界面效应。

Result: 临界电流密度j_crit超过后阳极电压呈指数增长而非线性；三种电解质的活化能E_A为0.13-0.15 eV，显著高于体Na中空位扩散活化能（0.053 eV）；E_A对阳极微观结构变化不敏感；锡-钠合金中间层将E_A降至(0.10±0.01) eV，表明界面热力学是速率限制因素。

Conclusion: 钠金属陶瓷固态电池的脱层问题主要由界面热力学而非体扩散控制；亲钠、钠导电中间层和低张力界面是实现稳定、高倍率钠固态电池的关键途径，特别是在实际堆叠压力下。

Abstract: Ceramic solid-state batteries with sodium (Na) metal electrodes promise enhanced safety and energy density compared to contemporary secondary batteries. However, the critical delamination of the Na metal electrode during discharge - when vacancies accumulate at the Na/ceramic interface - remains to be understood and avoided. The study investigates the underlying mechanism by applying a linear current ramp between two Na metal electrodes separated by a ceramic solid electrolyte to provoke vacancy buildup. Above a critical current density $j_\mathrm{crit}$ the anode voltage no longer increases linearly but in an exponential fashion. Arrhenius analysis of $j_\mathrm{crit}(T)$ for the three solid electrolytes $\mathrm{Na_{1.9}Al_{10.67}Li_{0.33}O_{17}}$, $\mathrm{Na_{3.4}Zr_2Si_{2.4}P_{0.6}O_{12}}$, and $\mathrm{Na_5SmSi_4O_{12}}$ yields an activation energy $E_\mathrm{A}$ of $0.13$ to $0.15\,\mathrm{eV}$. This exceeds the activation energy of $0.053\,\mathrm{eV}$ for the diffusive vacancy migration in bulk Na significantly. Further, $E_\mathrm{A}$ is insensitive to anode microstructure variation. Both observations rule out bulk diffusion as the transport bottleneck. A thin tin-sodium alloy interlayer lowers $E_\mathrm{A}$ to $(0.10\pm0.01)\,\mathrm{eV}$, implicating interfacial thermodynamics as rate-limiting. Sodiophilic, Na-conducting interlayers and low-tension interfaces emerge as key pathways to stable, high-rate Na-SSBs at practical stack pressures.

</details>


### [101] [Reliable and High Performance IGZO and In2O3 Transistors via Channel Capping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23341)
*C. W. Cheng,J. Smith,K. Mashooq,P. Solomon,R. Watters,T. Philicelli,D. Piatek,C. Lavoie,M. Hopstaken,L. Gignac,B. Khan,M. BrightSky,G. Gionta,P. Hashemi,V. Narayanan,M. M. Frank*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 演示了一种与400°C BEOL热预算兼容且无性能退化的可靠IGZO和In₂O₃晶体管器件与工艺策略，通过充分利用氧化物材料固有特性实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发与后端工艺(BEOL)兼容的可靠氧化物半导体晶体管，解决传统封装方法在高温工艺下的性能退化问题

Method: 采用新型非晶In₂O₃与SiO₂混合覆盖层结构，充分利用氧化物材料的固有特性，实现与400°C BEOL热预算兼容的工艺

Result: In₂O₃晶体管表现出正阈值电压、33.1 cm²/V·s的高外饱和迁移率，在3 MV/cm正偏压应力下1000秒后仅5mV Vt偏移，性能优于传统SiO₂封装

Conclusion: 通过优化材料组合和工艺策略，成功实现了与BEOL工艺兼容的高性能可靠氧化物晶体管，为先进半导体制造提供了可行方案

Abstract: A device and process strategy for achieving reliable indium gallium zinc oxide and indium oxide transistors compatible with a 400oC BEOL thermal budget and without performance degradation is demonstrated by fully exploiting intrinsic oxide material properties. An indium oxide transistor with a novel amorphous In2O3 mixed with SiO2 capping layer exhibits a positive threshold voltage, high extrinsic saturation mobility 33.1 cm2/V.s ,and only a 5mV Vt shift after positive-bias stress at 3 MV/cm for 1000s at room temperature, superior to conventional SiO2 encapsulation.

</details>


### [102] [Ultrafast Sintering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23423)
*Jian Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文综述了超快烧结技术的最新进展，评估了多种超快烧结方法及其机理，并指出了该领域存在的科学问题和未来技术机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在批判性地评估超快烧结技术的最新进展，揭示该领域的科学问题和新兴技术机遇。随着材料科学的发展，特别是高熵陶瓷和成分复杂陶瓷的出现，需要高效、快速的烧结方法来探索广阔的成分空间。

Method: 采用综述性分析方法，系统评估了多种超快烧结技术：包括快速热退火（使用强红外加热）、超快高温烧结（样品夹在石墨毡加热器之间）、黑光烧结（采用蓝色激光或强紫外辐照）、大气压等离子体烧结和感应超快烧结（直接感应或感受器加热模式）。同时探讨了反应性超快合成与烧结。

Result: 研究表明，闪烧事件始于热和电耦合的失控过程，而快速致密化则通过超高加热速率和升高烧结温度实现。超快烧结已通过多种方法实现，反应性超快合成与烧结也已得到验证。然而，超快烧结的机理和动力学仍需进一步研究。

Conclusion: 超快烧结方法为高通量材料发现提供了多功能平台，特别适用于快速发展的成分复杂陶瓷领域。反应性超快合成与烧结在科学上具有吸引力，同时提供了技术机遇。未来需要深入研究超快烧结的机理，特别是在高熵和成分复杂陶瓷中的应用。

Abstract: This Perspective critically assesses recent advances in ultrafast sintering and highlights open scientific questions and emerging technological opportunities. Mechanistic studies of flash sintering indicate that the flash event initiates as a coupled thermal and electrical runaway, while rapid densification is enabled by ultrahigh heating rates and elevated sintering temperatures. Building on this understanding, ultrafast sintering has been realized through multiple approaches, including rapid thermal annealing (using intense infrared heating), ultrafast high-temperature sintering (in which specimens are sandwiched between graphite felt heaters), black light sintering (employing blue laser or intense ultraviolet irradiation), atmospheric-pressure plasma sintering, and induction ultrafast sintering (operating in either direct induction or susceptor-heating modes).Reactive ultrafast synthesis and sintering have also been demonstrated. Although several hypotheses have been proposed, the mechanisms governing ultrafast sintering and its kinetics warrant further investigation. In particular, reactive ultrafast synthesis and sintering of compositionally complex ceramics are scientifically intriguing to understand while also presenting technological opportunities. The expanding range of ultrafast sintering methods provides a versatile platform for high-throughput materials discovery, especially in the rapidly growing field of high-entropy and compositionally complex ceramics, which feature vast compositional spaces to explore.

</details>


### [103] [Ferromagnetic Spin Glass State and Anomalous Hall Effect in Topological Semimetal Candidate Mn2Sb2Te5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23426)
*M. M. Sharma,Ankush Saxena,S. M. Huang,Santosh Karki Chhetri,Jin Hu,V. P. S. Awana*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Mn2Sb2Te5单晶表现出自旋玻璃态和场致铁磁性，具有反常霍尔效应，是研究磁性与拓扑态相互作用的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究同时具有磁性和拓扑态的材料是量子材料研究的前沿。Mn2(Bi/Sb)2Te5体系作为潜在的磁性外尔半金属，有望实现拓扑表面态与内禀磁序的耦合。

Method: 通过磁化测量、热容测量和电输运性质研究，分析了Mn2Sb2Te5单晶的磁性状态和电子性质。

Result: 磁化测量显示自旋玻璃态和场致铁磁性；热容测量表明缺乏长程有序；反常霍尔效应证实内禀磁化显著影响电学性质。

Conclusion: Mn2(Bi/Sb)2Te5体系为探索磁性与非平庸能带拓扑的相互作用提供了理想平台，能够实现非磁性系统中无法获得的量子相和新型输运响应。

Abstract: Materials that intrinsically possess both magnetism and topological states represent a key frontier of quantum materials research. Recently, Mn2(Bi/Sb)2Te5 has emerged as a promising candidate for hosting topological surface states coupled with intrinsic magnetic order, making it a potential magnetic Weyl semimetal. In this study, we investigate the magnetic and transport properties of Mn2Sb2Te5 single crystals. The magnetization measurements reveal a spin glass state with field-induced ferromagnetism. Although heat capacity measurement indicates the absence of long-range order, the intrinsic magnetization in Mn2Sb2Te5 significantly affects its electrical properties, as demonstrated by the anomalous Hall effect. This work provides valuable insights into the magnetism and the electronic properties of Mn2Sb2Te5, establishing Mn2(Bi/Sb)2Te5 system as a compelling platform for exploring the interplay between magnetism and non-trivial band topology, enabling emergent quantum phases and novel transport responses not accessible in non-magnetic systems.

</details>


### [104] [Structural Chart of Copper-Silver Nanoalloys through machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23442)
*Manoj Settem,Emanuele Telari,Antonio Tinti,Riccardo Ferrando,Alberto Giacomello*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 开发了一个计算框架来构建38原子AgCu纳米合金的结构图谱，通过并行回火分子动力学采样和机器学习分类，可视化不同结构和成分，揭示了纳米合金与块体合金在热稳定性上的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米合金作为功能材料具有重要应用前景，但合成过程中控制其结构和化学有序性面临挑战，主要困难在于缺乏对其亚稳态和稳定结构的了解。

Method: 开发通用计算框架：1) 使用并行回火分子动力学(PTMD)采样38原子AgCu纳米合金的平衡结构分布；2) 采用机器学习方法对大量采样构型进行分类；3) 生成三维图谱可视化所有结构和成分；4) 构建有限温度结构图谱显示不同成分和温度下的主导结构。

Result: 成功构建了38原子AgCu纳米合金的结构图谱，能够可视化区分不同结构和成分。结构图谱揭示了纳米合金与块体合金在热稳定性上存在显著差异，为多组分纳米颗粒的结构和化学空间提供了有效计算和映射方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为多组分纳米颗粒的广阔结构和化学空间提供了有效的计算和映射途径，为功能纳米合金的理性设计铺平了道路。

Abstract: Nanoalloys (or alloy nanoparticles) are an important class of materials that are promising for their functional properties. However, designing synthesis protocols to control their structure and chemical ordering is rather challenging. Part of this difficulty stems from the lack of information on their metastable and stable structures. Here, we develop a general computational framework to construct a structural chart of nanoalloys using 38-atom AgCu nanoalloys as a model system. Initially, the equilibrium structural distribution is sampled using parallel tempering combined with molecular dynamics (PTMD). Using a machine learning (ML) based approach, the vast number of sampled configurations are classified into various structural classes. This ML approach produces a single three-dimensional map in which all structures and compositions can be visualized and discriminated. Finally, a finite-temperature structural chart is constructed which provides information on the dominant structures across the entire range of compositions and temperatures. In addition, the structural chart reveals significant differences in thermal stability between nanoalloys and bulk alloys. The presented framework provides an effective route to compute and map the vast structural and chemical space of multicomponent nanoparticles, paving the way to the rational design of functional nanoalloys.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum Saturation of the Electro-Optic Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23486)
*Aiden Ross,Sankalpa Hazra,Albert Suceava,Dylan Sotir,Darrell G. Schlom,Venkatraman Gopalan,Long-Qing Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过调控铁电相边界至0K，利用量子涨落实现低温下大且温度不敏感的电光效应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算架构需要能在低温下保持强稳定性能的电光材料，传统材料的大电光系数通常局限于结构相变附近的窄温度窗口

Method: 采用热力学分析、相场模拟、实验生长和低温光学测量，通过应变调控BaTiO3或化学组分调控Ba1-xCaxTiO3来调节铁电相边界

Result: 在25K以下实现了大且几乎温度不敏感的电光响应，性能超过室温块体BaTiO3，比BaTiO3-on-Si高一个数量级以上

Conclusion: 建立了一种通用设计原则，用于工程化适用于低温应用的高性能电光材料

Abstract: Future quantum computing architectures require electro-optic materials that maintain a strong, stable performance at cryogenic temperatures. In conventional electro-optic materials, large electro-optic coefficients are often confined to narrow temperature windows near structural phase transitions, where small changes in temperature lead to large changes in the electro-optic response. Using thermodynamic analysis, phase-field simulations, experimental growth and cryogenic optical measurements we show that quantum fluctuations can be harnessed to overcome this trade-off. By tuning the ferroelectric phase boundaries down to 0 K, quantum fluctuations induce a saturation regime in which a large electro-optic response becomes nearly temperature-independent below 25 K. We demonstrate that the phase boundaries can be tuned through either strain in BaTiO3 or through chemical composition in Ba1-xCaxTiO3, leading to a large, temperature insensitive, cryogenic electro-optic effect comparable to bulk BaTiO3 at room temperature; the performance exceeds BaTiO3-on-Si by over an order of magnitude. These findings establish a general design principle for engineering high-performance electro-optic materials for cryogenic applications.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [How active field theories couple to external potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22424)
*Yariv Kafri,Julien Tailleur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统微扰展开，确定了活性场论中耦合外部势能所需的最简项，揭示了密度与势能梯度之间的非平凡耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究活性场论中耦合外部势能所需的最简项，以解释活性粒子在外部势能作用下的非平衡特征，如边界积累和远场密度调制等现象。

Method: 采用活性布朗粒子模型，在粒子持续时间内进行系统微扰展开，推导出密度与势能梯度之间的耦合项。

Result: 发现了密度与势能梯度之间的非平凡耦合关系，该耦合能够解释活性粒子在外部势能作用下的非平衡行为，包括边界积累和远场密度调制。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广到具有成对相互作用的粒子和推进速度空间调制的系统，为理解活性物质在外部势能作用下的非平衡行为提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study the simplest terms that need to be included in active field theories to couple them to external potentials. To do so, we consider active Brownian particles and implement a systematic perturbative expansion in the particle persistence time. The result is a non-trivial coupling between density and potential gradients, which accounts for the nonequilibrium features of active particles in the presence of an external potential, from boundary accumulation to far-field density modulation. We show how the method can be applied to particles interacting via pairwise forces and to spatial modulations of the propulsion speed.

</details>


### [107] [The damage spreading transition: a hierarchy of renormalization group fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22439)
*Adam Nahum,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文发展了损伤传播相变的理论，揭示了其比定向渗流更丰富的层次结构，包含无限多个局部可观测量层次，每个层次都有额外的普适临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典确定性细胞自动机根据动力学规则的可逆性可分为强不可逆相和弱不可逆相，两者之间的损伤传播相变通常被认为与定向渗流相关。然而，作者发现损伤传播临界点的完整理论比定向渗流更丰富，需要发展更全面的理论框架。

Method: 作者发展了损伤传播相变的完整理论，揭示了其包含无限层次结构的局部可观测量。使用场论表述和重正化群论证分析这些高阶固定点，并在1+1维度进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 损伤传播临界点包含无限层次的局部可观测量，定向渗流仅描述第一层次。高阶可观测量包括多个轨迹的"重叠"，可由集合划分标记。这些高阶固定点都包含定向渗流作为子扇区，但具有额外的普适临界指数。

Conclusion: 损伤传播相变的理论比定向渗流更丰富，形成了一个无限层次的固定点层次结构，每个层次都有独特的临界行为，为研究不可逆动力学中的熵衰减等问题提供了更完整的理论框架。

Abstract: Deterministic classical cellular automata can be in two phases, depending on how irreversible the dynamical rules are. In the strongly irreversible phase, trajectories with different initial conditions coalesce quickly, while in the weakly irreversible phase, trajectories with different initial conditions can remain different for a time exponential in the system volume. The transition between these phases is referred to as the damage-spreading transition (the "damaged" sites are those that differ between the trajectories). We develop a theory for this transition. In the simplest and most generic setting, the transition is known to be related to directed percolation, one of the best-studied nonequilibrium phase transitions. However, we show that full theory of the damage-spreading critical point is richer than directed percolation, and contains an infinite hierarchy of sectors of local observables. Directed percolation describes the first level of the hierarchy. The higher observables include "overlaps" for multiple trajectories, and may be labeled by set partitions. (These higher observables arise naturally if, for example, we consider decay of entropy under the irreversible dynamics.) The full hierarchy yields a hierarchy of nonequilibrium fixed points for reaction-diffusion-type processes, all of which contain directed percolation as a subsector, but which possess additional universal critical exponents. We analyze these higher fixed points using a field theory formulation and renormalization group arguments, and using simulations in 1+1 dimensions.

</details>


### [108] [Geometric Thermodynamics of Cycles: Curvature and Local Thermodynamic Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22559)
*Eric R. Bittner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文将经典热力学循环的几何关系统一为平衡热力学流形上的单一正则二形式，建立了循环几何与热力学响应之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典热力学中，机械功与(P,V)平面中轨迹包围的面积相关，但缺乏统一的几何框架来描述热力学循环在不同表示（如(P,V)和(T,S)）中的关系。作者旨在建立一个统一的几何描述，将循环几何与热力学响应联系起来，并将经典循环几何扩展到非平衡热力学轨迹。

Method: 作者在平衡热力学流形上定义了一个单一的正则二形式，通过该二形式的投影得到功和可逆热的面积定律。利用该几何结构，建立了循环几何与热力学响应之间的联系，特别是将微小循环产生的功与平衡能面的混合曲率U_SV联系起来。该框架还将经典循环几何与随机热力学轨迹相连接。

Result: 研究结果表明：(1) 功和可逆热的面积定律可以统一为单一正则二形式的投影；(2) 微小循环产生的功由平衡能面的混合曲率U_SV决定，该曲率可以用可测量的响应函数表示；(3) 热力学功被识别为状态空间上的局部几何场，而不仅仅是循环过程的全局性质；(4) 该框架将经典循环几何与非平衡功关系（如Jarzynski等式）联系起来。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个统一的几何框架，将经典热力学循环的几何描述与热力学响应理论相连接。通过将热力学功重新解释为状态空间上的局部几何场，该框架不仅统一了不同表示中的循环描述，还为理解非平衡热力学关系（如Jarzynski等式）提供了几何解释，从而连接了经典热力学与随机热力学。

Abstract: Classical thermodynamics contains familiar geometric relations associated with cyclic processes, most notably the identification of mechanical work with the area enclosed by a trajectory in the $(P,V)$ plane. We show that the area laws for work and reversible heat arise as projections of a single canonical two--form defined on the equilibrium thermodynamic manifold, providing a unified description of thermodynamic cycles in both the $(P,V)$ and $(T,S)$ representations. The same structure yields a direct link between cycle geometry and thermodynamic response: the work generated by infinitesimal cycles is set locally by the mixed curvature $U_{SV}$ of the equilibrium energy surface, which can be expressed in terms of measurable susceptibilities. This identifies thermodynamic work as a local geometric field over state space rather than solely a global property of cyclic processes. More broadly, the framework connects classical cycle geometry to stochastic thermodynamic trajectories, providing a geometric interpretation of nonequilibrium work relations such as the Jarzynski equality.

</details>


### [109] [Pseudospectral phenomena and the origin of the non-Hermitian skin effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22643)
*J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）与点隙拓扑之间的联系，指出特征谱对边界条件和扰动高度敏感，而拓扑性质反映在奇异值谱中。研究表明NHSE源于谱不稳定性和非互易性而非拓扑，点隙缠绕与边界局域化之间的常见关系依赖于平移对称性而非普适。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）与点隙拓扑之间的联系，挑战传统观点认为NHSE源于布洛赫哈密顿量的非平凡点隙拓扑。作者认为特征谱对边界条件和扰动高度敏感，不是稳定的拓扑信息载体。

Method: 通过分析非正规算子的特征谱敏感性，提出拓扑性质反映在有限系统的奇异值谱中。在半无限极限下，对应Toeplitz算子的指标隐含边界局域化本征模。使用Hatano-Nelson梯子模型，独立变化点隙缠绕和非正规性，验证NHSE与点隙缠绕的分离关系。

Result: 证明NHSE可以在没有点隙缠绕的情况下发生，反之点隙缠绕也可以在没有NHSE的情况下持续存在。表明NHSE源于谱不稳定性和非互易性而非拓扑性质。传统假设的谱缠绕与边界局域化关系依赖于平移对称性，不具有普适性。

Conclusion: 非厄米趋肤效应（NHSE）的起源是谱不稳定性和非互易性，而非拓扑性质。拓扑信息应通过奇异值谱而非特征谱来表征。点隙缠绕与边界局域化之间的常见关系不是普适的，而是依赖于平移对称性这一特定条件。

Abstract: The non-Hermitian skin effect (NHSE), characterized by a macroscopic accumulation of eigenstates at the edge of a system with open boundaries, is often ascribed to a non-trivial point-gap topology of the Bloch Hamiltonian. We revisit this connection and show that the eigenspectrum of non-normal operators is highly sensitive to boundary conditions and generic perturbations, and therefore does not constitute a stable object encoding topological information. Instead, topological properties are reflected in the singular-value spectrum of finite systems and, in the semi-infinite limit, correspond to boundary-localized eigenmodes implied by the index of the corresponding Toeplitz operator. For a Hatano-Nelson ladder, where point-gap winding and non-normality can be varied independently, we demonstrate that the NHSE can occur without point-gap winding and, conversely, that point-gap winding can persist without the NHSE. These results establish that the NHSE originates from spectral instability and non-reciprocity rather than topology, and that the commonly assumed relation between spectral winding and boundary localization relies on translational invariance and is therefore not generic.

</details>


### [110] [Genuine and spurious (non-)ergodicity in single particle tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22989)
*Wei Wang,Qing Wei,Igor M. Sokolov,Ralf Metzler,Aleksei Chechkin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文指出传统MSD-TAMSD准则在判断遍历性时存在缺陷，提出使用MSI替代MSD进行时间平均与系综平均比较的新准则，能更准确识别弱遍历破缺和超弱遍历破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单粒子追踪实验中，判断随机过程的遍历性对理解异常扩散动力学至关重要。传统基于MSD与TAMSD比较的遍历性判断准则虽然广泛应用，但其理论依据和局限性缺乏深入讨论，在某些情况下会得出错误的遍历性结论。

Method: 提出使用均方增量（MSI）替代均方位移（MSD）作为系综平均可观测量，与时间平均MSD（TAMSD）进行比较，建立MSI-TAMSD准则。通过多个经典随机模型验证该方法的有效性。

Result: MSI-TAMSD准则不仅能有效揭示弱遍历破缺（与MSD-TAMSD方法等效），还能更准确地表征MSD-TAMSD方法失效系统的真实遍历性。对于"超弱"遍历破缺系统，MSI能揭示增量过程的渐近平稳性和遍历性。

Conclusion: MSI可观测量在单粒子追踪实验和异常扩散研究中具有重要作用，提出的MSI-TAMSD准则为遍历性判断提供了更准确的理论框架，解决了传统MSD-TAMSD方法的局限性。

Abstract: In single-particle tracking experiments measuring anomalous diffusion dynamics, understanding ergodicity is crucial, as it ensures that the time average of an observable matches the ensemble average, and can thus be fitted with known ensemble-averaged observables. A commonly used criterion for assessing the ergodicity of a stochastic process is based on the comparison of the mean-squared displacement (MSD) with the time-averaged MSD (TAMSD). This approach has been widely applied and proves effective in cases of weak ergodicity breaking across various systems in both theoretical and experimental studies. However, there is relatively little discussion regarding the theoretical justification and limitations of this definition. Here, we demonstrate that this widely accepted criterion to some extent contradicts the classical definition of ergodicity as well as physical intuition, leading to spurious (non-)ergodicity results when applied to several well-known stochastic models. To address this limitation, we propose using the mean-squared increment (MSI) instead of the MSD for comparison of ensemble- and time-averaged observables. Several well-established examples demonstrate that our MSI-TAMSD criterion not only effectively reveals weak ergodicity breaking, equivalent to the MSD-TAMSD approach, but also provides a more accurate characterization of the genuine (non-)ergodicity of systems where the MSD-TAMSD method fails. Additionally, for systems exhibiting "ultraweak" ergodicity breaking, the MSI can reveal the asymptotic stationarity and ergodic nature of the process' increments. Our findings emphasize the important role of the MSI observable for SPT experiments and anomalous diffusion studies.

</details>


### [111] [Dynamics of Aligning Active Matter: Mapping to a Schrödinger Equation and Exact Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23021)
*Tara Steinhöfel,Horst-Holger Boltz,Thomas Ihle*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究重新审视Fokker-Planck与Schrödinger方程的经典联系，通过精确对角化方法分析小规模全连接自驱动对齐粒子系统的弛豫模式，改进现有近似结果，并将方法扩展到非互易相互作用系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Spera等人近期研究的小规模全连接自驱动对齐粒子系统的弛豫模式问题，作者希望建立更严格的解析框架，改进现有线性化统计场论的近似结果，并探索非互易相互作用对系统动力学的影响。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程与Schrödinger方程的经典联系，采用精确对角化方法分析系统全谱，推导渐近正确的解析结果，并将方法扩展到非互易相互作用系统，研究非厄米Schrödinger问题。

Result: 获得了比现有线性化统计场论更精确的解析结果；证明了即使非互易性不改变稳态分布，也能从根本上改变稳态性质（通过熵产生量化）；分析了小粒子数和大粒子数极限下的系统行为。

Conclusion: 该方法为小规模全连接自驱动对齐粒子系统提供了严格的解析框架，能够改进现有近似并扩展到非互易相互作用系统，揭示了非互易性对稳态性质的深刻影响，为研究开放量子力学类问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: There has been recent interest in the relaxational modes of small-scale fully connected systems of aligning self-propelled particles (Spera et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. {\bf 132}: 078301 (2024)). We revisit the classical connection between Fokker-Planck and Schrödinger equations to address this by means of exact diagonalization, allowing for rigorous analytical insight into the full spectrum. This allows us to extract exact results which we compare to the existing result from linearized statistical field theory. We derive asymptotically correct analytical results that improve upon the prior approximations. We show that this methodology can fruitfully be extended to the case of non-reciprocal interactions which gives rise to a non-Hermitian Schrödinger problem akin to those in open quantum mechanics. While the non-reciprocity can be chosen such as not to alter the stationary distribution, it fundamentally changes the nature of the steady state which we quantify via the entropy production. We discuss the case of low particle numbers as well as the emergence of mean-field dynamics at large numbers.

</details>


### [112] [Basis dependence of eigenstate thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23058)
*Lennart Dabelow,Christian Eidecker-Dunkel,Peter Reimann*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究表明，在具有简并性的多体系统中，本征态热化假设的成立与否强烈依赖于本征基的选择，不同基下热化本征态的比例可以完全不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究简并系统中本征态热化假设对基选择的依赖性，探索对称性如何影响简并性和热化行为。

Method: 通过构建简单模型展示不同基选择下热化本征态比例的差异；证明空间平移和反射对称性必然导致简并性；推导本征态热化对基选择依赖性的普遍界限。

Result: 发现一个基中热化本征态比例在热力学极限下趋于零（弱本征态热化假设成立），而另一个基中该比例保持非零（假设被违反）；证明平移和反射对称性必然产生简并；建立了基依赖性的一般界限。

Conclusion: 本征态热化假设的成立与基选择密切相关，对称性导致的简并性对此有重要影响，这对理解多体系统的热化行为和弛豫特性有深远意义。

Abstract: Eigenstate thermalization refers to the property that an energy eigenstate of a many-body system is indistinguishable from a thermal equilibrium ensemble at the same energy as far as expectation values of local observables are concerned. In systems with degeneracies, the choice of an energy eigenbasis is not unique and the fraction of basis states exhibiting eigenstate thermalization can vary. We present a simple example where this fraction vanishes in the thermodynamic limit for one basis choice, but remains nonzero for another choice. In other words, the weak eigenstate thermalization hypothesis is satisfied in the first, but violated in the second basis. We furthermore prove that degeneracies must abound whenever a system is simultaneously symmetric under spatial translations and reflection. Finally, we derive general bounds on how strongly eigenstate thermalization may depend on the choice of the basis, and we reveal some interesting implications regarding the temporal relaxation properties of such systems.

</details>


### [113] [Amplification based on the noise-induced negative differential resistance in a Zener diode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23169)
*Alexandre Dumont,Bertrand Reulet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过噪声反馈在12V齐纳二极管反向偏置区诱导负微分电阻，构建音频范围电压放大器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电压偏置齐纳二极管始终呈现正微分电阻，无法用作信号放大元件。需要探索在齐纳二极管中实现负微分电阻的方法以构建放大器。

Method: 采用噪声反馈技术在12V齐纳二极管的反向偏置区诱导负微分电阻现象，并基于此构建音频频率范围的电压放大器。

Result: 成功实现了负微分电阻效应，构建的放大器在音频范围内工作，提供了带宽、增益、功耗、增益压缩和输出噪声谱密度等完整特性参数。

Conclusion: 噪声反馈技术能够有效在齐纳二极管中诱导负微分电阻，使其可用于构建实用的电压放大器，突破了传统齐纳二极管只能作为正电阻元件的限制。

Abstract: A voltage biased Zener diode always exhibit positive differential resistance, thus cannot be used as an element to provide amplification of a signal. We show how to induce negative differential resistance in the reverse bias regime of a 12V Zener diode by noise feedback. We use this to build a voltage amplifier in the audio frequency range, which we characterize by providing bandwidth, gain, power consumption, gain compression and output noise spectral density.

</details>


### [114] [Dynamics of O(2) excitations in a non-reciprocal medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23225)
*Ylann Rouzaire,Daniel JG Pearce,Ignacio Pagonabarraga,Demian Levis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了O(2)模型中非互易性引起的涌现动力学，建立了非互易性与活性之间的关联，并推导了描述激发轨迹控制的连续介质理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非互易性在O(2)模型中的动力学效应，特别是视觉锥状耦合产生的非互易性如何影响系统模式形成和激发演化，旨在建立非互易系统与活性系统之间的理论联系。

Method: 采用晶格XY模型，其中非互易性源于视觉锥状耦合；通过连续介质描述将非互易性转化为取向场旋度相关的新项；推导广义Burgers方程描述一维激发，并扩展到二维扰动分析。

Result: 建立了非互易O(2)模型的连续介质描述，揭示了非互易性与活性的等价性；推导了激发轨迹控制的基本原理，展示了非互易程度和背景介质取向对激发演化的调控作用；发现了非零缠绕数无缺陷激发的稳定性变化。

Conclusion: 非互易性在O(2)模型中产生类似活性的动力学效应，能够主动平流和重塑模式；建立了非互易系统中激发轨迹控制的第一性原理，为理解非互易介质中的模式演化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate emergent dynamics due to non-reciprocity in the $\mathcal{O}(2)$ model. The lattice XY model, where non-reciprocity stems from vision cone like couplings, can be described by a continuum description in which non-reciprocity translates into a new term depending on the rotational of the orientation field. We argue that non-reciprocity is akin to activity and we highlight the connection between our hydrodynamic equation and the constant density Toner-Tu framework. The active force advects and reshapes patterns, a generic feature found in many non-reciprocal systems. We show how $1d$ excitations in the non-reciprocal $\mathcal{O}(2)$ model can be described by a generalized Burgers equation, derived from our continuum model. We then extend the results to $2d$ perturbations. As such, we establish the first principles of excitation trajectory control in a non-reciprocal $\mathcal{O}(2)$ medium. Concretely, we explain how tuning the degree of non-reciprocity and the orientation of the background medium impacts the time evolution of excitations. We also showcase how initially different excitations lead to very different dynamical behavior. Non-reciprocity also affects the stability of defect-free excitations with non-zero winding numbers and, unlike in its equilibrium $O(2)$ counterpart, enables the system, above a certain threshold, to relax to its ground state.

</details>


### [115] [A $q$-Caputo Fractional Generalization of Tsallis Entropy: Series Representation and Non-Negativity Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23254)
*Matias P. Gonzalez,Micolta-Riascos Bayron*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过q-Caputo算子对生成函数∑p_i^x在x=1处作用，引入Tsallis熵的分数阶推广，得到S_q^α熵，并研究其非负性区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将分数阶微积分概念引入非广延统计力学，扩展Tsallis熵框架，探索分数阶参数α对熵性质的影响。

Method: 使用q-Caputo分数阶微分积分算子（0<α<1）作用于生成函数∑p_i^x在x=1处，推导出用q-Gamma函数表示的闭合级数表达式。

Result: 当α→1时恢复标准Tsallis熵S_q；通过数值计算确定了分数阶参数α和非广延指数q对熵非负性（或负值）的影响区域。

Conclusion: 成功构建了Tsallis熵的分数阶推广S_q^α，该构造在极限情况下具有良好行为，并揭示了分数阶参数对熵符号的影响。

Abstract: We introduce a fractional generalization of Tsallis entropy by acting with a $q$-Caputo operator on the generating family $\sum_i p_i^{\,x}$ evaluated at $x=1$. Concretely, we define $S_{q}^α$ through the $q$-Caputo differintegral of order $0<α<1$ and derive a closed series representation in terms of the $q$-Gamma function. The construction is anchored at the evaluation point, which ensures well-behaved limits: as $α\!\to\!1$ we recover the standard Tsallis entropy $S_q$. Finally we perform a numerical calculation to show the regions where the obtained $q$-fractional entropy $S^α_q$ can be non-negative (or negative) through the fractional parameter $α$ and the non extensive index $q$.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Cotunneling theory and multiplet excitations: emergence of asymmetric line shape in inelastic scanning tunneling spectroscopy of correlated molecules on surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22505)
*Marco Lozano,Manish Kumar,Pavel Jelinek,Diego Soler-Polo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了共隧穿理论到多参考系统，揭示了轨道依赖性和不对称耦合如何导致扫描隧道谱中的不对称线形


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描隧道谱实验中经常观察到费米能级附近的不对称峰和谷，传统理论基于有效模型哈密顿量解释对称的阶梯状特征，但缺乏对不对称线形的微观机制解释

Method: 将共隧穿形式主义扩展到一般的多参考系统，考虑轨道依赖性和强不对称的尖端/衬底耦合效应

Result: 多参考特性结合轨道依赖性和强不对称耦合可以在非弹性扫描隧道谱中产生显著的不对称线形，为实验观察提供新的微观机制解释

Conclusion: 该工作为扫描隧道谱中常见的不对称特征提供了基于多参考系统和不对称耦合的替代解释框架，扩展了表面分子磁激发谱的理论理解

Abstract: Recent advances in on-surface chemistry, combined with scanning probe microscopy, have enabled the synthesis of correlated molecules on surfaces and the characterization of their chemical and electronic properties with unprecedented spatial resolution. Low-energy magnetic excitations of individual molecules are frequently investigated by scanning tunneling spectroscopy (STS) and often appear as symmetric step-like features in the differential conductance as a function of bias voltage. The interpretation of such steps is well established within cotunneling theory and effective model Hamiltonians (e.g., Hubbard- and spin-based models). Here, we extend the cotunneling formalism to general multireference systems. We show that multireference character, together with orbital-dependent and strongly asymmetric tip/substrate couplings, can produce pronounced asymmetric line shapes in inelastic STS. These results provide an alternative microscopic mechanism for the asymmetric peaks and dips near the Fermi level frequently observed in STS experiments.

</details>


### [117] [Magnetic Weyl semimetals: Interplay of band topology and magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22568)
*Akihiro Ozawa,Yasufumi Araki,Koji Kobayashi,Kentaro Nomura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文综述了磁性Weyl半金属的最新理论和实验进展，重点分析了其电子能带拓扑与磁性相互作用产生的电磁响应现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性Weyl半金属结合了拓扑电子态和磁性，为研究拓扑与磁性的相互作用提供了独特平台，具有重要的基础物理意义和潜在的低耗散电子学、自旋电子学应用价值。

Method: 通过理论综述和实验总结的方法，系统分析了磁性Weyl半金属的拓扑性质、磁性机制、非均匀磁结构、磁化动力学以及磁输运现象。

Result: 综述了磁性Weyl半金属的多种电磁响应现象，包括反常霍尔效应、手征磁效应、自旋动力、自旋力矩、畴壁磁阻等，并列举了铁磁、反铁磁等不同磁有序的材料体系。

Conclusion: Weyl电子的拓扑性质不仅对基础物理研究至关重要，而且为开发低耗散电子学和自旋电子学器件提供了潜在应用前景，磁性Weyl半金属是这一研究领域的核心平台。

Abstract: We review recent theoretical and experimental developments in magnetic Weyl semimetals, focusing on the electromagnetic responses emerging from the interplay of their electronic band topology and magnetism. We begin by introducing the fundamental topological properties of the electrons in Weyl semimetals, and provide an overview of the characteristic phenomena arising from their band topology, such as the anomalous Hall effect and chiral magnetic effect. The materials exhibiting the magnetic Weyl semimetal state, with ferromagnetic ordering, antiferromagnetic ordering, etc., are listed. The possible mechanisms for their magnetism are discussed in connection with the Weyl electrons. Non-uniform magnetic textures and magnetization dynamics are expected to exhibit a topological interplay with the Weyl electrons, manifesting as spinmotive force and spin torques. We also review the magnetotransport phenomena such as domain wall magnetoresistance, studied by mesoscopic scale calculations. Finally, we mention the spin transport properties studied in magnetic Weyl semimetals. The topological nature of Weyl electrons reviewed here is important not only for fundamental physics, but also for the potential application to low-dissipative electronics and spintronics devices.

</details>


### [118] [Two-dimensional bound excitons in the real space and Landau quantization space: a comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22715)
*Kunxiang Li,Yi-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过实空间和朗道量子化空间分析单层WSe₂中二维激子的性质，发现磁场和库仑相互作用会影响激子态中电子-空穴对的主导成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朗道量子化空间基于电子和空穴在磁场中的相对运动，为理解实验中观察到的束缚激子行为提供了新途径。研究旨在通过实空间和朗道量子化空间分析单层WSe₂中二维激子的性质，特别是零质心动量激子的能谱和特性。

Method: 在实空间和朗道量子化空间中研究单层WSe₂的二维激子性质。聚焦于零质心动量激子，计算两个空间中的能谱，并比较结果的一致性。获取抗磁系数和均方根半径，并与实验中的s态结果对比。分析激子态nl中电子-空穴对主导成分随磁场和库仑相互作用的变化规律。

Result: 实空间和朗道量子化空间计算的能谱结果吻合良好。获得的抗磁系数和均方根半径与实验中的s态结果一致。研究发现激子态nl中电子-空穴对的主导成分会随磁场和库仑相互作用变化：磁场驱动主导成分成为自由电子-空穴对{n_e=n+l-1, n_h=n-1}，而库仑相互作用则驱动其成为较低指数的电子-空穴对。

Conclusion: 朗道量子化空间为理解二维激子性质提供了有效框架。磁场和库仑相互作用对激子态中电子-空穴对的主导成分有显著影响，磁场倾向于产生较高指数的自由电子-空穴对，而库仑相互作用则倾向于较低指数的配对。这些发现有助于深入理解二维材料中激子的量子行为。

Abstract: The Landau quantization space is based on the respective motion of the electron and hole in a magnetic field and can provide a new route to understand the bound exciton behaviors observed in the experiments. In this paper, we study the two-dimensional exciton properties of monolayer WSe$_2$ in both the real space and Landau quantization space. Focusing on the excitons of zero center-of-mass momentum, we calculate its energy spectrum in both spaces, with the results agreeing well with each other. We then obtain the diamagnetic coefficients and root-mean-square radius, which are consistent with the $s$ state results from the experiments. More importantly, in the exciton state $nl$, we find that the dominant electron-hole pair component may shift with the magnetic field and the Coulomb interactions, and reveal that the magnetic field will drive the dominant component to be the free electron-hole pair $\{n_e=n+l-1,n_h=n-1\}$, whereas the Coulomb interactions drives it to be the pair of the lower index.

</details>


### [119] [Electron scattering by a magnetic monopole in solid-state experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22865)
*P. S. Sidorov,N. A. Vlasov,I. S. Terekhov,A. I. Milstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出研究二维电子气中磁单极场中电子散射的实验方案，在eikonal近似下获得微分散射截面，发现与无限长螺线管场的结果一致，并证明散射电子会产生自旋极化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁单极场中电子散射的物理特性，特别是探索在二维电子气系统中磁单极场对电子散射和自旋极化的影响

Method: 提出实验方案，采用eikonal近似计算微分散射截面，分析非极化初始电子的散射行为，并与无限长螺线管场进行对比

Result: 微分散射截面与无限长螺线管场结果一致（需磁通量重新定义）；非极化初始电子散射后会产生自旋极化；在类霍尔实验装置中，自旋极化方向垂直于电子电流方向

Conclusion: 磁单极场中的电子散射会产生独特的自旋极化效应，这为二维电子气系统中的磁单极物理研究提供了新的实验途径和理论框架

Abstract: The scheme of experiment for studying electron scattering in the field of a magnetic monopole in two dimensional electron gas is proposed. The differential scattering cross section is obtained in the eikonal approximation. For unpolarized initial electron, the differential cross section coincides with that in the field of an infinitely long solenoid, up to redefinition of a magnetic flux. It is shown that the scattered electron becomes polarized even for unpolarized initial electron. Besides, in an experimental setup similar to the Hall experiment, the spin polarization arises in the direction perpendicular to the electron current.

</details>


### [120] [Solitary waves in a phononic integrated circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22898)
*Timothy M. F. Hirsch,Xiaoya Jin,Nicolas P. Mauranyapin,Nishta Arora,Erick Romero,Matthew Reeves,Glen I. Harris,Warwick P. Bowen,Christopher G. Baker*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在集成声子波导中产生声学孤子，实现了对暗孤子动力学的精确观测，包括数百次孤子碰撞、分裂和Wigner晶体熔化，验证了暗孤子的碰撞相移和两种深度依赖碰撞机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤子作为普遍的非线性激发，在强非线性区域和频繁相互作用下的动力学行为尚未得到充分实验探索。现有研究受限于同时实现色散和非线性精确控制以及时空分辨率的困难。

Method: 利用集成声子波导，通过波导色散与机械Kerr非线性的相互作用产生声学暗孤子，利用慢声子速度实现米级传播距离的直接成像观测。

Result: 观测到数百次暗孤子碰撞（比先前多两个数量级）、孤子分裂和孤子Wigner晶体熔化。首次实验验证了暗孤子的碰撞相移和两种深度依赖碰撞机制。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅阐明了基本的非线性能量传输过程，还为声学版本的孤子技术（如频率梳和锁模激光器）提供了发展路径。

Abstract: Solitons are universal nonlinear excitations that appear in settings as varied as optics, water waves, and quantum gases [1-5]. While reduced models of soliton dynamics are well established, their validity and dynamical behaviour in strongly nonlinear regimes with frequent interactions remain largely unexplored experimentally. Progress has been constrained by the difficulty of simultaneously achieving precise control of dispersion and nonlinearity, together with the temporal and spatial resolution required for dynamical observations. Here we overcome these difficulties by producing acoustic solitons in integrated phononic waveguides. We exploit the interplay between waveguide dispersion and mechanical Kerr nonlinearity to generate 'dark' solitons that persist over metre-scale propagation distances. The slow phonon velocity allows direct imaging of hundreds of dark soliton collisions -- two orders of magnitude more than have previously been accessible [6, 7] -- as well as soliton fission and the melting of a soliton Wigner crystal. Furthermore, the unprecedented dynamical resolution allows us to verify two long-predicted aspects of dark soliton behaviour: the existence of a collisional phase shift and two depth-dependent collision regimes [8, 9]. These results not only illuminate fundamental nonlinear energy transport processes, but also show a path towards acoustic versions of soliton-enabled technologies such as frequency combs and mode-locked lasers [1, 2, 10].

</details>


### [121] [Charge Transport Modeling of CdSe/ZnS core/shell Quantum Nanorod Light-Emitting Diodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22961)
*A. G. Melkonyan,G. A. Mantashian,D. B. Hayrapetyan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过自洽数值方法分析了CdSe-ZnS核壳双纳米棒量子点发光二极管的电子结构、电荷传输动力学和光学特性，揭示了电压调控的电子局域化动力学和量子隧穿机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双纳米棒量子点发光二极管（QD-LED）的电子结构、电荷传输和光学特性，探索外部偏压作为调控参数对器件性能的影响，为可调谐光电子和光子应用提供理论基础。

Method: 采用严格的自洽数值方法，迭代求解耦合的薛定谔-泊松方程，获得精确的波函数、能级和电势分布；使用非对称厄朗分布表征界面效应；计算电流-电压特性和光致发光光谱。

Result: 揭示了电压依赖的电子局域化动力学，展示了电子通过量子隧穿在不同纳米棒区域间的系统转移；外部电压能够有效调控发射能量和强度，证明了这些纳米棒-LED系统在可调谐光电子应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 双纳米棒QD-LED系统具有显著的电压可调谐性，外部偏压可作为强大的调控参数来调制发射特性，为开发高性能可调谐光电子和光子器件提供了重要指导。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate the electronic structure, charge transport dynamics, and optical properties of a quantum dot light-emitting diode (QD-LED) featuring a double nanorod (NR) emission layer composed of CdSe-ZnS core-shell structures. Utilizing a rigorous self-consistent numerical approach, we solve the coupled Schrodinger-Poisson equations iteratively to obtain accurate wave functions, energy levels, and potential profiles under varying external bias voltages. Detailed analyses reveal voltage-dependent electron localization dynamics, demonstrating a systematic transition of electrons between distinct NR regions via quantum tunneling. Charge density and electrostatic potential distributions are modeled comprehensively, employing the asymmetric Erlang distribution to characterize interface effects. By calculating current-voltage (I-V) characteristics and photoluminescence spectra, we demonstrate that external voltage serves as a robust tuning parameter for modulating emission energies and intensities, underscoring the potential of these NR-LED systems for tunable optoelectronic and photonic applications.

</details>


### [122] [Ultrastrong Coupling and Coherent Dynamics in a Gate-Tunable Transmon Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19438)
*I. Casal Iglesias,F. J. Matute-Cañadas,G. O. Steffensen,A. Ibabe,L. Splitthoff,T. Kanne,J. Nygard,V. Rollano,D. Granados,A. Gomez,R. Aguado,A. Levy Yeyati,E. J. H. Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在InAs纳米线gatemon量子比特与超导谐振器的混合系统中实现了超强光-物质耦合，并展示了该体系下的相干时间分辨控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超强光-物质耦合（USC）能实现奇异的量子现象并有望实现更快的量子门操作，但该体系下的相干时域控制仍未被充分探索。

Method: 使用InAs纳米线基gatemon量子比特与超导谐振器构建混合系统，通过光谱学测量避免交叉和光子数依赖的跃迁，并实现时间分辨的相干控制。

Result: 光谱学显示无法用Jaynes-Cummings模型描述的避免交叉，以及明显偏离强耦合体系预期的光子数依赖跃迁；实现了量子比特的相干控制，相干时间与工作在非USC体系的gatemons相当。

Conclusion: 混合半导体-超导量子比特在超强耦合体系中能保持相干控制，为探索该体系的量子动力学和器件概念提供了平台。

Abstract: Ultrastrong light-matter coupling (USC) gives access to exotic quantum phenomena and promises faster quantum gates, yet coherent time-domain control in this regime remains largely unexplored. Here, we realize USC in a hybrid system consisting of an InAs nanowire-based gatemon qubit coupled to a superconducting resonator. Spectroscopy reveals an avoided crossing that cannot be captured by the Jaynes-Cummings (JC) model, as well as photon-number-dependent transitions whose energies deviate markedly from the JC ladder expected in the strong coupling regime. Beyond demonstrating USC, we achieve time-resolved coherent control of the qubit and measure coherence times comparable to gatemons operating outside the USC regime. These results establish that hybrid semiconductor-superconductor qubits can retain coherent control in USC and provide a platform for exploring quantum dynamics and device concepts in this regime.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum correlations and dissipative blockade of polaritons in a tunable fiber cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23002)
*Gian-Marco Schnüriger,Martin Kroner,Emre Togan,Patrick Knüppel,Aymeric Delteil,Stefan Fält,Werner Wegscheider,Atac Imamoglu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过连续波共振激光激发下的光子关联测量，展示了腔极化激元之间的量子关联，揭示了极化激元相互作用对腔-激子失谐的强烈依赖性，并发现了在双极化激元Feshbach共振条件下的耗散阻塞机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究腔极化激元（量子阱激子与腔模杂化形成的准粒子）之间的量子关联特性，特别是探索极化激元相互作用对腔-激子失谐的依赖性，以及在不同共振条件下的量子统计行为。

Method: 采用连续波共振激光激发，进行光子关联测量（photon correlation measurements）。通过调节腔-激子失谐，研究极化激元在不同条件下的量子统计特性。当极化激元主要为激子样时，观察激光调谐跨越极化激元共振时的光子反聚束到聚束转变；当调节下支极化激元能量以诱导双极化激元Feshbach共振时，研究极化激元反聚束程度的变化。

Result: 实验发现极化激元相互作用对腔-激子失谐有出乎意料的强烈依赖性。当极化激元主要为激子样时，观察到光子从反聚束到聚束的转变，与简单的Kerr非线性模型一致。当存在双极化激元Feshbach共振时，极化激元反聚束程度变得与激光失谐无关，这归因于大双激子展宽引起的耗散阻塞机制。研究还表明，通过将极化激元衰减率降低10倍，可以实现强极化激元阻塞区域。

Conclusion: 该研究成功展示了腔极化激元之间的量子关联，揭示了极化激元相互作用对腔-激子失谐的强烈依赖性，并发现了在Feshbach共振条件下的耗散阻塞机制。这些发现为在腔极化激元系统中实现强量子关联和量子信息处理应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Cavity exciton--polaritons are quasiparticles that form when quantum well excitons hybridize with a cavity mode. Here, we carry out photon correlation measurements under continuous wave resonant laser excitation to demonstrate quantum correlations between cavity--polaritons. Our experiments reveal an unexpectedly strong dependence of polariton interactions on cavity--exciton detuning. When the polaritons are predominantly exciton-like, we observe a transition from photon antibunching to bunching as the laser is tuned across the polariton resonance, in agreement with a simple Kerr-nonlinearity model. When the lower-branch polariton energy is tuned to induce a two-polariton Feshbach resonance with the biexciton mode, the degree of polariton antibunching becomes independent of the laser detuning: we explain our finding by invoking a dissipative blockade mechanism arising from large biexciton broadening. Our experiments demonstrate that the strong polariton blockade regime would be achieved by reducing the polariton decay rate by a factor of 10.

</details>


### [124] [Pre-Patterned Superconducting Contacts for Clean Superconductor-Topological Material Interfaces Enabling Long-Range Josephson Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23148)
*Yong-Bin Choi,Chang-Won Choi,Luke Holtzman,Hoil Kim,Seongwoo Kang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,James Hone,Jun Sung Kim,Si-Young Choi,Gil-Ho Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种预图案化超导底接触架构，通过在范德华晶体转移前定义MoRe/Au电极，避免了传统顶接触制造中的界面退化问题，在拓扑材料约瑟夫森结中实现了更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超导-拓扑材料（SC-TM）界面制造方法（顶接触）常因氧化、聚合物残留和工艺诱导无序导致界面退化，影响相位相干超导邻近效应的质量，需要更清洁的界面制造技术。

Method: 采用预图案化超导底接触架构：在范德华晶体转移前预先定义MoRe/Au电极，避免转移后的片上光刻工艺，通过横截面STEM/EDS表征界面原子结构和化学成分。

Result: 在WTe2和Bi1.5Sb0.5Te1.7Se1.3基约瑟夫森结中，预图案化底接触相比传统顶接触表现出：1）系统性地更大的I_c R_N乘积；2）更长程的耦合；3）横截面STEM/EDS显示原子级陡峭、化学分离良好的界面。

Conclusion: 预图案化SC-TM接触为范德华拓扑材料中可重复、微米级约瑟夫森平台提供了一条实用路径，能够实现高质量的超导-拓扑材料界面。

Abstract: Phase-coherent superconducting proximity in topological materials requires clean superconductor-topological material (SC-TM) interfaces, yet conventional top-contact fabrication often degrades them through oxidation, polymer residue, and process-induced disorder. Here we introduce a pre-patterned superconducting bottom-contact architecture in which MoRe/Au electrodes are defined before van der Waals crystal transfer, thereby avoiding on-flake lithography after transfer. In WTe2- and Bi1.5Sb0.5Te1.7Se1.3-based Josephson junctions, this architecture yields systematically larger I_c R_N and longer-ranged coupling than conventional top contacts. Cross-sectional STEM/EDS reveals atomically abrupt, chemically well-separated interfaces. These results establish pre-patterned SC-TM contacts as a practical route to reproducible, micrometer-scale Josephson platforms in van der Waals topological materials.

</details>


### [125] [A $Γ$-valley Moiré Platform for Tunable Square Lattice Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23174)
*Rui Shi,Kejie Bao,Huan Wang,Jing Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: Γ-谷扭曲方晶双层结构为模拟t-t'-U Hubbard模型提供了高度可调平台，通过层间位移场调控有效跳跃比t'/t


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moiré超晶格是模拟Hubbard模型的理想平台，但在方晶格系统中实现高可调性仍面临挑战。本文旨在证明Γ-谷扭曲方晶同质双层结构能够忠实且高度可调地实现t-t'-U Hubbard模型

Method: 利用Γ-谷扭曲方晶同质双层结构，在小扭转角下通过涌现的层交换对称性将电子态解耦到两个嵌套的方子晶格上，再通过层间位移场破坏该对称性以诱导可控的子晶格间杂化

Result: 建立了Γ-谷和M-谷系统之间的直接对应关系，为理解方晶Moiré物理中的位移场可调性提供了统一框架，实现了有效跳跃比t'/t的宽范围实验调控

Conclusion: Γ-谷扭曲双层结构成为模拟方晶格Hubbard模型和探索其丰富关联现象的通用平台，扩展了先前M-谷系统的研究

Abstract: Moiré superlattices have emerged as a premier platform for simulating the Hubbard model, yet achieving high tunability in square-lattice systems remains a key challenge. We demonstrate that $Γ$-valley twisted square homobilayers provide a faithful and highly tunable realization of $t-t'-U$ Hubbard model, extending the recent proposal in M-valley systems. We show that at small twist angles, an emergent layer-exchange symmetry decouples electronic states into flat bands residing on two nested square sublattices. An interlayer displacement field breaks this symmetry to induce controllable inter-sublattice hybridization, enabling wide-range experimental tuning of the effective hopping ratio $t'/t$. By establishing a direct correspondence between $Γ$- and M-valley systems, we provide a unified framework for understanding displacement-field tunability in square moiré physics. These findings establish $Γ$-valley twisted bilayers as a versatile platform for simulating the square-lattice Hubbard model and exploring its rich landscape of correlated phenomena.

</details>


### [126] [Enhanced spin-current generation in Dirac altermagnets through Klein tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23235)
*Tomas T. Osterholt,Lumen Eek,Cristiane Morais Smith,Rembert A. Duine*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了狄拉克反铁磁体中的自旋电流生成，发现克莱因隧穿可作为增强自旋输运的有效机制，并探索了l波对称性对自旋电流极化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体作为自旋电子学新平台，具有自旋分裂能带但净磁化强度为零的特性。本文旨在探索狄拉克反铁磁体中自旋电流的生成机制，特别是克莱因隧穿在增强自旋输运方面的潜力。

Method: 采用低能狄拉克模型结合散射理论，分析克莱因隧穿在反铁磁体中的自旋依赖性。通过调节势垒高度、宽度和方向来控制电子自旋电流极化，并研究d波和g波狄拉克反铁磁体的l波对称性如何影响自旋电流极化和透射。

Result: 发现反铁磁体中的克莱因隧穿具有强自旋依赖性，可用于有效控制电子自旋电流极化。g波狄拉克反铁磁体表现出特别有前景的结果：势垒存在可显著增强自旋电流极化，即使自旋分裂能带结构产生的本征极化极小。通过静电门控实现的势垒机制允许通过栅极电压开关自旋电流极化。

Conclusion: 克莱因隧穿是狄拉克反铁磁体中增强自旋输运的有效机制，通过势垒工程可实现对自旋电流极化的控制。g波狄拉克反铁磁体尤其具有应用潜力，其自旋电流极化可通过栅压进行电控开关，为自旋电子学器件设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Altermagnets have recently emerged as a new platform for spintronics applications, offering spin-split electronic bands despite vanishing net magnetization. Here, we investigate spin-current generation in Dirac altermagnets and identify Klein tunneling as an efficient mechanism for enhancing spin transport. Using a low-energy Dirac model combined with scattering theory, we demonstrate that Klein tunneling in altermagnets is strongly spin-dependent and can be used to effectively control the electronic spin-current polarization by, for instance, adjusting the height, width and orientation of the potential barrier. Finally, we explore how the l-wave symmetry of the Dirac altermagnet shapes the spin-current polarization and transmission, focusing especially on the d- and g-wave cases. Particularly promising results are obtained for the g-wave Dirac altermagnet, as it is found that the presence of a potential barrier can significantly boost the spin-current polarization, even when the intrinsic polarization due to the spin-split band structure is vanishingly small. For a barrier implemented via electrostatic gating, such a mechanism would in turn allow the spin-current polarization to be switched on and off via a gate voltage.

</details>


### [127] [Tunable Goos--Hänchen shifts and group delay time in single-barrier silicene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23264)
*Youssef Fattasse,Hocine Bahlouli,Clarence Cortes,David Laroze,Ahmed Jellal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究硅烯中狄拉克费米子穿越矩形静电势垒时的古斯-汉欣位移和群延迟时间，分析其与入射角、势垒高度、宽度和能量的关系，发现量子干涉效应导致显著振荡特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维狄拉克材料中静电势垒如何通过量子干涉效应调控狄拉克费米子的横向空间位移（古斯-汉欣位移）和时间延迟特性，探索在硅烯中实现可调谐输运的潜力。

Method: 通过理论分析硅烯中狄拉克费米子穿越矩形静电势垒的量子输运过程，计算古斯-汉欣位移和群延迟时间，系统研究这些物理量对入射角、势垒高度、势垒宽度和入射能量的依赖关系。

Result: 古斯-汉欣位移表现出明显的振荡行为，振幅和振荡峰数量随能量、势垒宽度和入射角增加而增加，导致横向束流位移增强；群延迟时间呈现与准束缚态形成相关的共振特征，随势垒宽度、能量和入射角增加而增加，但随势垒高度增加而减小。

Conclusion: 势垒诱导的量子干涉效应有效控制了硅烯中狄拉克费米子的横向空间位移和时间延迟动力学，表明静电势垒在二维狄拉克材料中具有实现可调谐输运的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the Goos--Hänchen (GH) shifts and group delay time of Dirac fermions traversing a rectangular electrostatic potential barrier in silicene. By analyzing their dependence on the incident angle, barrier height, barrier width, and incident energy, we demonstrate that the GH shifts exhibit pronounced oscillations arising from quantum interference within the barrier region. The amplitude and number of oscillation peaks increase with increasing energy, barrier width, and incidence angle, resulting in enhanced lateral beam displacement. Meanwhile, the group delay time exhibits resonant features associated with the formation of quasi-bound states, increasing with barrier width, energy, and incidence angle, while decreasing with increasing barrier height. These results clarify how barrier-induced quantum interference controls both the lateral and temporal dynamics of Dirac fermions in silicene, highlighting the potential role of electrostatic barriers in enabling tunable transport in two-dimensional Dirac materials.

</details>


### [128] [Comment on 'Observation of Shapiro Steps in the Charge Density Wave State Induced by Strain on a Piezoelectric Substrate'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23274)
*D. Yu. Saltykova,M. V. Nikitin,V. Ya. Pokrovskii,S. G. Zybtsev,V. V. Kolesov,V. V. Kashin,I. E. Kuznetsova,I. A. Nedospasov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: NbSe3纳米线中电荷密度波滑动与表面声波同步的实验研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电荷密度波（CDW）系统与外部周期性驱动（表面声波）之间的同步现象，探索CDW动力学与声学模式的耦合机制

Method: 在LiNbO3压电衬底上使用叉指换能器（IDT）激发表面声波（SAW），测量NbSe3纳米线在SAW作用下的I-V特性曲线，观察Shapiro台阶的出现

Result: 当激发SAW模式时，I-V曲线上出现Shapiro台阶；当频率偏离共振时，没有观察到Shapiro台阶，这为CDW与声学模式的同步提供了明确证据

Conclusion: 实验成功证明了NbSe3纳米线中电荷密度波滑动可以与表面声波同步，为研究CDW系统与外部周期性驱动的耦合提供了新途径

Abstract: In their Letter Fujiwara et al. (10.48550/arXiv.2511.09888. 2025) report a high-quality experiment demonstrating the synchronization of the CDW sliding in NbSe3 whiskers (nanowires) with surface acoustic waves (SAWs). The SAWs are induced in the conventional LiNbO3 piezoelectric substrates through application of rf voltage to an interdigital transducer (IDT). When a SAW mode is excited, Shapiro steps (ShSs) appear on the I-V curves, while no ShSs are observed when the frequency is shifted from the resonance. This gives clear evidence of synchronization of the CDW with the acoustic modes.

</details>


### [129] [Strain-Engineered Deterministic Quantum Dots for Telecom O-Band Emission Using Buried Stressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23392)
*Imad Limame,Ching-Wen Shih,Kartik Gaur,Martin Podhorský,Sarthak Tripathi,Setthanat Wijitpatima,Aris Koulas-Simos,Chirag C. Palekar,Petr Klenovský,Stephan Reitzenstein*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用埋入式AlAs/Al₂O₃应力层实现电信O波段发射的位点控制InGaAs/GaAs量子点，无需应变缓冲层，兼具空间控制和光谱调谐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现电信波长量子光源的确定性制备对于长距离光纤量子通信和分布式量子计算至关重要。传统方法需要应变缓冲层，但会降低光学相干性。

Method: 采用埋入式AlAs/Al₂O₃应力层，在生长表面产生可控的拉伸应变场，使量子点发射红移至~1.3μm范围，并在台面中心实现位点选择性成核。通过8波段k·p和组态相互作用模型分析激子态发射特性。

Result: 生长的电信量子点显示纯单光子发射，g²(τ)在4K时为(5.0±1.0)×10⁻²，77K时为(2.8±0.3)×10⁻¹。理论模型定量复现实验观测，并提出多埋入应力层策略可将发射进一步红移至O波段中心及更远。

Conclusion: 埋入应力层概念为高度相干、位置可控的O波段量子发射器提供了一条可扩展路径，兼容工业光子集成，是实验与理论相结合的成功框架。

Abstract: The deterministic realization of quantum light sources operating at telecom wavelengths is essential for long-distance fiber-based quantum communication and distributed quantum computing. In this work, we demonstrate that telecom O-band emission can be achieved from site-controlled InGaAs/GaAs quantum dots (QDs). Our concept utilizes a buried AlAs/Al$_2$O$_3$ stressor layer with the unique feature that induces a well-defined and controllable tensile strain field at the growth surface, enabling both a redshift of QD emission to the $\sim$1.3~μm range and site-selective nucleation at the mesa centers. This concept eliminates not only the need for strain-reducing layers (SRLs), which are known to degrade optical coherence, but also provides spatial control and spectral tunability. The grown telecom QDs show pure single-photon emission with $g^{(2)}(τ) = (5.0 \pm 1.0) \times 10^{-2}$ at 4 K and $(2.8 \pm 0.3) \times 10^{-1}$ at 77~K, demonstrating the quantum nature and thermal stability of the emitters. The emission characteristics of complex excitonic states are analyzed using 8-band $k \cdot p$ and configuration-interaction modeling, which quantitatively reproduces the experimental observations. Finally, we present a theory-supported strategy to further redshift the emission toward the center of the O-band and beyond by employing a multi-buried-stressor approach. This combined framework of experiment and theory establishes the buried stressor concept as a scalable route toward highly coherent, position-controlled O-band quantum emitters compatible with industrial photonic integration.

</details>
